ChronoSentiment

Decision Observatory

Observatory Decision Feed Policy

No early peek. No retrospective edits. Decisions are sealed at decision time. Outcomes become visible only when their observation window closes. ChronoSentiment is not predicting the future on this screen; it is preserving the boundary between a decision and its future evidence.

Replay integrity is not strategy validation. Historical replay is a backtesting mechanism. This replay is not yet a statistical strategy backtest. The observation horizon is part of the decision-observation contract.

293 decisions · 230 LONG · 63 SHORT · 0 NO TRADE · 91 completed · 202 observing · 0 outcome due · 47 positive V · 44 negative V

Candidate C3-002 · paper only. This is an evidence dashboard, not a performance dashboard. Winners and losers stay visible. IDEA and MAHABANK remain. Mean, median, and total decision value are not homepage metrics. Historical path: 91 sealed / 91 observed — lifecycle PASS, not a profitability claim. Historical replay is a backtesting mechanism. Outcomes are not known at T and are not attached. Not CS-P-003 validation. C.3-G is untouched. Search #3 is not authorized.

Open decisions

Outcome not yet observed. OPEN / OBSERVING = evidence has not matured. OUTCOME DUE = window closed, observation not yet appended. COMPLETED = outcome known. Policy and decision stay immutable. Observation is append-only.

C3-002

Candidate Research Policy

Product label
ChronoSentiment Research Policy — Candidate C3-002
Artifact
5a43b9df97daa76d85edd7f7ef1c12c3a230ef292f7ecfa98ef9587647392121
Decisions
293
Observations
91
Capital
Paper only
Promotion
Not a strategy. Not Decision Engine v1.0. Immutable artifact.

Three layers

Intelligence
Coralys discovers a decision policy. C3-002 is frozen.
Decision integrity
What was known, what was decided, which policy, when. Decision and policy are immutable.
Evidence
What happened afterward, and the resulting decision value. Observation is append-only.

ChronoSentiment creates timestamped, auditable decisions from a defined information state and measures the outcome afterward. It does not predict stocks. A strategy claim can emerge only from accumulated prospective evidence. Research (C.3-F frozen, C.3-G a question) stays off the customer path. The seven-name universe is not expanded.

  1. POLICY — immutable artifact 5a43b9df97daa76d85edd7f7ef1c12c3a230ef292f7ecfa98ef9587647392121
  2. DECISION — immutable record; sealed_status stays OPEN
  3. OBSERVING — outcome not yet observed; 20 market sessions countdown
  4. OUTCOME DUE — window closed; observation not yet appended
  5. OBSERVATION — append-only; observation_status COMPLETED
  6. COMPLETED / OBSERVED — outcome known; decision unchanged
